2026-10-17T21:17:02.6579031Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Build the Decode MegaMoE kernel patches into the image and serve them with static Prefill expert placement

A1 of the Decode kernel + Prefill placement bundle. Against main's
reference config (concurrency 192, P3/D1, every worker TP4/DP4/EP4 with
MegaMoE, decode cap 128, EAGLE3 steps 3 / draft 4) the image gains three
Decode-only DeepGEMM patches and the Prefill workers load a static expert
placement. Nothing else changes.

Why bundle: in P3/D1 the throughput is set by Prefill supply while Decode
already runs at the ITL limit, so a faster Decode alone mostly turns into
fewer ITL > 20 ms failures and a faster Prefill alone (placement run
39053527142, 6418.50, Prefill MegaMoE -8.1%) raised ITL failures. Together
they should raise good output throughput. Because same-config runs differ
by about 3% (6521.98 vs 6699.30), the bundle is judged on two runs of this
image (A1, A2) interleaved with a reference rerun on image e29dfde (R2,
39069954937, commit 0b18a7c), not on one run.

Image change (source/patch change, hence [require-build]):
- patches/deep-gemm-bench/{sm90-decode-swap-ab-hoist,
  sm90-decode-swap-ab-l1-quant,sm90-dispatch-pull-overlap}.patch move to
  patches/deep-gemm/ unchanged (sha256 88351653..., 012220e3...,
  450d2749..., byte-identical to 901288a). containers/build-sm90-megamoe.py
  applies them after the existing five in the same order the kernel bench
  used (name order), scripts/build-sglang-container.sh copies them into the
  build context and containers/Dockerfile copies them to /opt/apac. The
  build record gains swap_ab_hoist_patch_sha256, l1_quant_patch_sha256 and
  dispatch_overlap_patch_sha256, and scripts/check-firmus-image.sh requires
  them plus the new header sha256 135a43be... (original 0.1.5 header
  a53d83ca... -> 8 patches; reproduced locally from both the original and
  the e29dfde image header). The image-metadata test pins the same values
  and covers a changed hash for each new patch.
- patches/deep-gemm-bench/ is now empty on this branch, so the kernel bench
  applies no candidate twice; the timeline overlay from 901288a already
  applies on top of the new header.
- Kernel bench evidence (research/runtime-performance/
  2026-10-18-megamoe-decode-mainloop-pipeline.md and
  2026-10-18-megamoe-decode-epilogue-dispatch.md): Decode-128 MegaMoE
  229.5 -> 200.8 us, all 12 output hashes bit-identical to the image run
  39053820377, Prefill SASS byte-identical, Decode 168 registers / 0 spills.

Runtime change (merged from a61d08d): prefill.expert_location points at
configs/firmus-prefill-expert-location.json and only the Prefill workers
get --init-expert-location; Decode keeps the trivial placement.

Cheap checks before this push: shell syntax, the line-continuation check,
the kernel-experiment tag check, ruff and the full pytest suite pass; the
15-patch SGLang chain applies to the pinned source fc275674 and
check-firmus-staging/megamoe-source/latency-source pass (including the
placement-mapped expert ids); the 8-patch DeepGEMM chain applies to the
original header and the timeline overlay applies on top.

Checks after the run: MegaMoE numerical gate PASS, official validation
valid, score / TTFT / ITL failures versus R2, Prefill and Decode MegaMoE
kernel means from the profile versus 2.163 / 0.2415 ms, server-info
static placement on Prefill and trivial on Decode, coherent sanity chat,
EAGLE3 acceptance unchanged.
